How to Tame a Multi-Headed Hydra? Adaptive Multi-Category Safety Steering for Large Language Models
Authors: Chenxi Wang, Ruiyang Huang, Li Huang, Yifan Wu
First: 2026-09-28T07:53:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-29T07:14:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) become increasingly widespread, preventing unsafe responses to harmful prompts is essential for their safe deployment. Activation steering offers an approach to improving LLM safety by modifying internal activations during inference without updating model parameters. However, a single prompt can involve multiple harm categories, and steering toward safety in one category may leave harmful content from another unaddressed. Despite advances in adaptive steering, existing methods do not explicitly coordinate steering direction and strength when multiple harm categories co-occur within a single prompt. To address this problem, we propose CAM-Steer, a Category-Adaptive Multi-category Safety Steering framework. Specifically, it estimates the risk associated with each harm category by comparing the current hidden state with safe and unsafe prototypes. The estimated risks are then used to combine the safety directions for different harm categories into a single steering direction and to determine the strength of the intervention. Finally, it rotates the hidden state along the composed steering direction, with the rotation angle determined by the estimated risks, while preserving the hidden-state norm. Experiments across three LLM backbones and seven harm categories show that CAM-Steer outperforms the evaluated baselines in average defense success rate, including when categories co-occur. Further analyses support its component designs and informative risk scores, with negligible inference overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如何驯服多头水蛇？大型语言模型的自适应多类别安全引导
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越普及，防止对有害提示的安全响应对于其安全部署至关重要。激活引导提供了一种通过在推理过程中修改内部激活而不更新模型参数来提高LLM安全性的方法。然而，单个提示可能涉及多个危害类别，而在一个类别中引导安全可能会忽视另一个类别的有害内容。尽管自适应引导有所进展，但现有方法在多个危害类别同时出现时并未明确协调引导方向和强度。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了CAM-Steer，一个类别自适应多类别安全引导框架。具体而言，它通过将当前隐藏状态与安全和不安全原型进行比较来估计与每个危害类别相关的风险。然后，估计的风险用于将不同危害类别的安全方向组合成一个单一的引导方向，并确定干预的强度。最后，它沿着组合的引导方向旋转隐藏状态，旋转角度由估计的风险决定，同时保持隐藏状态的范数。在三个LLM骨干和七个危害类别的实验中，CAM-Steer在平均防御成功率上优于评估的基线，包括类别共现时。进一步的分析支持其组件设计和信息丰富的风险评分，推理开销微乎其微。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical need for ensuring safety in large language models (LLMs) as they become more prevalent, particularly in preventing unsafe responses to harmful prompts. Previous methods, such as activation steering, modify internal activations during inference but struggle with prompts that involve multiple harm categories, often addressing only one category while neglecting others. The proposed CAM-Steer framework improves upon these methods by estimating risks for each harm category and integrating them into a unified steering direction, thus effectively managing multiple risks simultaneously. This approach is well-motivated as it enhances the safety of LLMs without requiring parameter updates. The methodology involves comparing hidden states with safe and unsafe prototypes to determine intervention strength and direction, leading to superior performance in defense success rates across various LLMs and harm categories, thereby supporting the goal of comprehensive safety in LLM deployment.
本研究关注大型语言模型（LLMs）安全部署的关键需求，旨在防止对有害提示的危险响应。以往的方法，尤其是激活引导，面临着在单个提示中管理多个危害类别的挑战，常常导致安全措施不完整。提出的CAM-Steer框架通过自适应估计与每个危害类别相关的风险，并协调引导方向和强度，以同时应对多重威胁，从而改进了这些方法。该方法的动机明确，因为它在不需要更新模型参数的情况下增强了安全性。该方法论涉及将隐藏状态与安全和不安全原型进行比较，以推导统一的引导方向和干预强度，然后在保持其范数的同时应用于隐藏状态。实验结果表明，CAM-Steer在各种LLM和危害类别中显著优于现有基线，在平均防御成功率上有效支持其预期的安全目标。
When Do Model Internals Help? Exploring the Role of Representation Engineering in LLM Safety
Authors: Tianyi Guan, Jianhui Chen, Liangming Pan
First: 2026-09-28T09:50:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-28T09:50:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable AI safeguards require both control mechanisms that reduce unsafe behavior and monitoring mechanisms that detect safety risks during model interactions. Established behavioral safeguards include alignment methods that optimize model outputs and text monitors that assess interaction text. Representation engineering instead reads or modifies internal model states, but the relative strengths of these approaches remain unclear because they are often evaluated under different settings. We present a matched evaluation across two tracks. For safety control, we compare DPO, a behavioral alignment method, with three representation steering methods across robustness, practicality, and granularity. DPO provides the strongest overall control and generally improves with increasing training data, although its safety can degrade after subsequent benign fine-tuning. Representation steering remains competitive primarily in low-data settings, particularly with high-quality contrastive data. For safety monitoring, we compare representation probes with fine-tuned and open-weight text monitors across full-response detection, early detection, and computational cost. Specialized text monitors achieve the strongest overall detection accuracy, while representation probes remain competitive at substantially lower marginal cost. Finally, monitor-guided interventions recover much of the safety lost by DPO after benign fine-tuning, with little additional over-refusal. Overall, representation engineering does not generally replace behavioral safeguards, but offers practical advantages under specific conditions and can provide complementary safety benefits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：模型内部何时有帮助？探索表示工程在大型语言模型安全性中的作用
可靠的人工智能安全措施需要控制机制以减少不安全行为，以及监测机制以在模型交互中检测安全风险。既定的行为安全措施包括优化模型输出的对齐方法和评估交互文本的文本监测器。表示工程则读取或修改内部模型状态，但这些方法的相对优势仍不清楚，因为它们通常在不同的设置下进行评估。我们在两个轨道上进行了匹配评估。对于安全控制，我们比较了行为对齐方法DPO与三种表示引导方法在鲁棒性、实用性和粒度方面的表现。DPO提供了最强的整体控制，并且通常随着训练数据的增加而改善，尽管在后续的良性微调后，其安全性可能会下降。表示引导在低数据设置中仍然具有竞争力，特别是在高质量对比数据的情况下。对于安全监测，我们比较了表示探针与微调和开放权重的文本监测器在完整响应检测、早期检测和计算成本方面的表现。专业文本监测器实现了最强的整体检测准确性，而表示探针在边际成本显著较低的情况下仍然具有竞争力。最后，监测引导的干预恢复了DPO在良性微调后失去的大部分安全性，几乎没有额外的拒绝。总体而言，表示工程通常并不取代行为安全措施，但在特定条件下提供了实用优势，并可以提供互补的安全益处。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the need for reliable AI safeguards that minimize unsafe behavior and detect safety risks during model interactions, highlighting the limitations of existing methods like alignment techniques and text monitors which are often evaluated in disparate contexts. The proposed approach, representation engineering, modifies internal model states and is evaluated alongside traditional methods in a matched setting, revealing that while DPO, a behavioral alignment method, excels in safety control, representation steering is effective in low-data scenarios. The paper contributes by providing a comparative analysis of these methods, demonstrating that specialized text monitors outperform representation probes in detection accuracy, yet the latter offers cost-effective alternatives. The methodology involves a dual evaluation of safety control and monitoring, achieving significant performance in safety interventions, particularly in recovering safety lost after benign fine-tuning, thus supporting the goal of enhancing AI safety mechanisms.
本研究关注于可靠的人工智能安全保障的需求，这些保障能够控制不安全行为并监测模型交互中的安全风险。以往的方法主要集中在行为对齐和文本监测上，这些方法在稳健性和适应性方面存在局限性，尤其是在低数据场景中。本文提出了一种匹配评估的表示工程方法，与传统方法并行，突出了它们在不同背景下的优缺点。研究方法包括比较行为对齐方法DPO与表示引导方法在安全控制方面的表现，以及表示探针与文本监测器在安全监测方面的表现。研究结果表明，尽管DPO通常提供更强的控制能力，但在低数据情况下，表示引导方法具有竞争力，而专门的文本监测器在检测准确性上优于表示探针。总体而言，研究表明表示工程可以在特定条件下补充现有的安全保障，增强人工智能系统的整体安全机制。
Easier Said Than Done: Unpacking Intent-Behavior Gap in Jailbreaking LLM-based Robots
Authors: Xuancun Lu, Zhengxian Huang, Xinfeng Li, Chi Zhang, Xiaoyu Ji, Wenyuan Xu
First: 2024-12-21T13:58:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-26T03:30:15+00:00
Comments: Accepted by NDSS 2027
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
LLM-based robots use Large Language Models (LLMs) as planners to translate natural language instructions into policies such as grasp(), move_to(), and open_gripper(). Jailbreak attacks on these robots extend the threat from generating malicious content to executing harmful behaviors. However, we find that existing jailbreak attempts against LLM-based robots that produce malicious-looking policies (intent jailbreaks) often fail to induce harmful physical actions by robots (behavior jailbreaks), due to robot-specific constraints, such as logical errors and hallucinated control APIs. In this paper, we demystify the intent-behavior gap and investigate its root causes to inform effective defenses. Our measurement study finds that current LLM jailbreak methods overlook robot-specific syntax constraints (e.g., executable control APIs) and physical feasibility (e.g., ordering of policies and hardware/kinematic constraints). To bridge the gap, we introduce POEF (Policy Effective Jailbreak), an automated red-teaming framework that takes into account the robot-specific constraints during both the optimization and evaluation processes. Specifically, POEF employs the hidden-layer gradients from an unaligned LLM to guide the jailbreak prompt optimization and uses a multi-agent evaluator to assess the feasibility of the generated policies. Experiments on commercial robots, including the Unitree G1, the Franka robotic arm, and simulators, show that POEF achieves an 80% behavior jailbreak success rate and transfers across various LLMs. In addition, we propose two defense strategies that mitigate the behavior jailbreak risks. Our findings indicate an urgent need for stronger countermeasures before LLM-based robots are deployed at scale. The homepage is available at https://zjushine.github.io/poef.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：说起来容易做起来难：揭示越狱 LLM 基于机器人的意图-行为差距
基于 LLM 的机器人使用大型语言模型 (LLM) 作为规划者，将自然语言指令转换为策略，如 grasp()、move_to() 和 open_gripper()。对这些机器人的越狱攻击将威胁从生成恶意内容扩展到执行有害行为。然而，我们发现，现有针对 LLM 基于机器人的越狱尝试（意图越狱）往往未能引发机器人（行为越狱）的有害物理动作，原因在于机器人特有的约束，如逻辑错误和虚构的控制 API。本文揭示了意图-行为差距，并调查其根本原因，以便为有效防御提供信息。我们的测量研究发现，当前的 LLM 越狱方法忽视了机器人特有的语法约束（例如，可执行的控制 API）和物理可行性（例如，策略的顺序和硬件/运动学约束）。为了解决这一差距，我们引入了 POEF（策略有效越狱），这是一个自动化红队框架，在优化和评估过程中考虑机器人特有的约束。具体而言，POEF 利用未对齐 LLM 的隐藏层梯度来指导越狱提示优化，并使用多智能体评估器来评估生成策略的可行性。在包括 Unitree G1、Franka 机械臂和模拟器在内的商业机器人上进行的实验表明，POEF 实现了 80% 的行为越狱成功率，并在各种 LLM 之间转移。此外，我们提出了两种防御策略，以减轻行为越狱风险。我们的研究结果表明，在 LLM 基于机器人大规模部署之前，迫切需要更强的对策。主页可访问 https://zjushine.github.io/poef.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the vulnerabilities of LLM-based robots to jailbreak attacks, which can lead to harmful behaviors despite existing methods primarily focusing on generating malicious-looking policies. Past approaches often fail due to overlooking robot-specific constraints such as logical errors and control API limitations, leading to a disconnect between intent and actual behavior. This paper contributes by introducing POEF (Policy Effective Jailbreak), an automated framework that incorporates these constraints during optimization and evaluation, resulting in an 80% success rate for behavior jailbreaks across various commercial robots and simulators. The methodology leverages hidden-layer gradients from unaligned LLMs for prompt optimization and employs a multi-agent evaluator to ensure the feasibility of generated policies, highlighting the need for improved defenses before widespread deployment of LLM-based robots.
本研究关注于LLM驱动的机器人面临的越狱攻击问题，这些攻击可能导致有害行为，尽管这些机器人被设计为遵循自然语言指令。以往的攻击方法常常因机器人特定的约束条件（如逻辑错误和控制API的局限性）而失败，导致意图与实际行为之间存在脱节。为了解决这些问题，作者提出了POEF（政策有效越狱），这是一种自动化的红队框架，在优化和评估阶段都考虑了机器人特定的约束条件。该方法显著提高了行为越狱的成功率，在各种商业机器人和模拟器上达到了80%的成功率，同时还提出了两种防御策略以减轻相关风险。研究结果强调了在LLM驱动的机器人广泛部署之前加强安全措施的必要性。
ArGuard Shared Task: Harmful Content Detection in Arabic Memes and LLM Prompts
Authors: Firoj Alam, Md. Rafiul Biswas, Mohamed Bayan Kmainasi, Ali Ezzat Shahroor, Hamdy Mubarak, George Mikros, Abul Hasnat, Wajdi Zaghouani
First: 2026-09-24T10:22:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-25T14:21:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
ArGuard is a shared task on harmful content detection in Arabic memes and LLM prompts. It includes two tracks: Track A focuses on multimodal hate detection in Arabic memes, while Track B addresses harmful prompt detection for Arabic LLM safety evaluation. In total, 58 teams registered, 35 participated in the final evaluation, and 27 submitted system-description papers. Participating teams explored models such as AraBERT, Jais, and Qwen3-VL. The best systems achieved macro-F1 scores of 0.823 on A1, 0.419 on A2, 0.984 on B1, and 0.790 on B2. Fine-grained meme classification in A2 was the most challenging setting, partly due to sparse labels and train-test distribution shifts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ArGuard共享任务：阿拉伯语表情包和LLM提示中的有害内容检测
ArGuard是一个关于阿拉伯语表情包和LLM提示中有害内容检测的共享任务。它包括两个赛道：A赛道专注于阿拉伯语表情包中的多模态仇恨检测，而B赛道则针对阿拉伯语LLM安全评估中的有害提示检测。共有58支队伍注册，35支参与了最终评估，27支提交了系统描述论文。参与的团队探索了AraBERT、Jais和Qwen3-VL等模型。最佳系统在A1上达到了0.823的宏F1分数，在A2上为0.419，在B1上为0.984，在B2上为0.790。A2中的细粒度表情包分类是最具挑战性的设置，部分原因是标签稀疏和训练-测试分布的变化。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the growing need for harmful content detection in Arabic digital media, particularly in memes and language model prompts, which has been underexplored in existing methodologies. Previous approaches often struggled with the complexities of multimodal data and the nuances of Arabic language, leading to inadequate detection performance. The proposed ArGuard shared task introduces a structured evaluation framework that includes two distinct tracks focusing on hate detection in memes and prompt safety evaluation, which effectively tackles the limitations of past methods. The methodology involved participation from 58 teams utilizing various models such as AraBERT and Jais, culminating in significant contributions to the field with the best systems achieving macro-F1 scores of 0.823 and 0.984 in their respective tracks, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed approach in enhancing harmful content detection in Arabic contexts.
本研究关注阿拉伯表情包和大型语言模型（LLM）生成的提示中有害内容的检测，强调在这一背景下有效检测方法的需求。以往的方法在处理多模态数据和阿拉伯语言的细微差别时缺乏针对性和稳健性，而所提出的方法通过利用AraBERT和Jais等先进模型来改善这一点。本文的贡献在于组织了ArGuard共享任务，评估各种系统在检测有害内容方面的能力，从而为未来的研究提供基准。该方法论包括两个轨道，分别关注表情包中的仇恨检测和提示安全评估，参与团队在有害内容检测中取得了高达0.984的宏F1分数，表明在尽管面临稀疏标签等挑战，仍能有效支持任务目标。
How Many Iterations to Jailbreak? Dynamic Budget Allocation for Multi-Turn LLM Evaluation
Authors: Shai Feldman, Yaniv Romano
First: 2026-05-07T17:25:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-24T19:53:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating and predicting the performance of large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings is critical yet computationally expensive; key events---e.g., jailbreaks or successful task completion by an agent---often emerge only after repeated interactions. These events might be rare and, under any feasible computational budget, remain unobserved. Recent conformal survival frameworks construct reliable lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the number of iterations to trigger the event of interest, but rely on static budget allocation that prohibits adaptivity in multi-turn setups. To address this, we introduce Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization (DAPRO), a theoretically valid dynamic budget allocation framework for bounding the time-to-event in multi-turn LLM interactions. We prove that DAPRO satisfies the expected budget constraint and provides distribution-free, finite-sample coverage guarantees without requiring the conditional independence between censoring and event times assumed by prior conformal survival approaches. A key theoretical contribution is a novel coverage bound that scales with the square root of the mean censoring weight over only a subset of the samples rather than the worst-case weight, yielding tighter guarantees than prior work. Furthermore, DAPRO can be employed to obtain unbiased estimates of population-level evaluation metrics, such as the jailbreak rate, under limited computing resources. Comprehensive experiments across agentic task success, LLM jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations demonstrate that DAPRO achieves coverage closer to the nominal level with lower variance than static baselines, while not exceeding the budget constraint in expectation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：越狱需要多少次迭代？多轮LLM评估的动态预算分配
在多轮对话环境中评估和预测大型语言模型（LLM）的性能至关重要，但计算成本高；关键事件——例如越狱或代理成功完成任务——通常仅在反复交互后出现。这些事件可能很少，并且在任何可行的计算预算下，仍然未被观察到。最近的保形生存框架构建了触发感兴趣事件所需迭代次数的可靠下限预测界（LPB），但依赖于静态预算分配，限制了多轮设置中的适应性。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了通过投影优化的动态分配（DAPRO），这是一个理论上有效的动态预算分配框架，用于界定多轮LLM交互中的事件发生时间。我们证明了DAPRO满足预期预算约束，并提供无分布、有限样本覆盖保证，而无需先前保形生存方法假设的删失时间与事件时间之间的条件独立性。一个关键的理论贡献是一个新的覆盖界限，它与仅在样本子集上计算的平均删失权重的平方根成比例，而不是最坏情况下的权重，从而提供比先前工作更紧的保证。此外，DAPRO可以在有限计算资源下获得人口级评估指标的无偏估计，例如越狱率。针对代理任务成功、LLM越狱、有毒内容生成和RAG幻觉的全面实验表明，DAPRO在不超过预期预算约束的情况下，达到了更接近名义水平的覆盖率，并且方差低于静态基线。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of evaluating large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversations, where key events like jailbreaks may only occur after numerous interactions, making traditional evaluation methods computationally expensive and often ineffective due to static budget allocation. Previous methods, particularly conformal survival frameworks, have limitations in adaptivity and rely on assumptions that may not hold in practical scenarios. The proposed Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization (DAPRO) framework offers a dynamic budget allocation approach that adheres to expected budget constraints while providing distribution-free coverage guarantees, thus improving upon the rigidity of past methods. This paper contributes a novel coverage bound that enhances the efficiency of event detection in LLM interactions and demonstrates that DAPRO can yield unbiased estimates of evaluation metrics under limited resources. Experimental results show that DAPRO achieves better coverage and lower variance compared to static methods while maintaining budget constraints, supporting its effectiveness in practical applications.
本研究解决了在多轮对话环境中评估大型语言模型（LLMs）的挑战，其中关键事件如越狱可能仅在多次交互后发生，这使得传统评估方法在计算上成本高昂且常常无效，因为它们依赖于静态预算分配。以往的方法，特别是符合生存框架，存在适应性不足的局限，并依赖于在实践中可能不成立的假设。所提出的动态分配通过投影优化（DAPRO）提供了一种动态预算分配框架，遵循预期预算约束，同时提供无分布覆盖保证，从而改善了早期方法的刚性。本文贡献了一种新颖的覆盖界限，增强了预测的可靠性，并允许在资源受限的情况下对评估指标进行无偏估计。实验结果表明，DAPRO在遵循预算限制的同时，能够实现比静态方法更好的覆盖率和更低的方差，从而支持其在实际应用中的有效性。
FinRED: An Expert-Guided Benchmark Generation and Evaluation Framework for Financial LLM Red-Teaming
Authors: Chaeyun Kim, Daeyoung Park, Junghwan Kim, Jinyoung Jeong, Eunji Song, Yongtaek Lim, Minwoo Kim
First: 2026-06-18T07:46:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-22T05:37:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE ICDM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Existing safety benchmarks target general adversarial scenarios but miss finance-specific risks. Financial LLMs face regulatory compliance violations, fraud facilitation, and systemic trust erosion that require targeted evaluation. We introduce FinRED, an expert-guided red-teaming framework for financial LLM safety evaluation developed with financial experts. FinRED uses a novel two-level taxonomy mapping global standards (e.g., FATF and EU DORA) to threats ranging from regulatory evasion to complex fraud, integrated with a scalable pipeline that converts real financial documents into context-rich red-teaming Behavioral Prompts (seeds) through an expert-defined schema. Rigorous expert validation confirms seed plausibility and realism for meaningful LLM safety evaluation. We also provide an expert-validated, finance-specific rubric that goes beyond disclaimer checks, aligns more closely with human experts than static one-size-fits-all rubrics, and reduces critical false negatives from 28 to 12. Aligned with internationally adopted risk-management and information-security standards (e.g., ISO/IEC 27001), FinRED is deployed in South Korea's Financial Security Institute (FSI) regulatory sandbox for generative AI security evaluation in real financial services. To mitigate dual-use risks, the dataset, generation pipeline, prompt template, and evaluation framework are gated for qualified researchers at https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper and https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED.

现有的安全基准针对一般对抗场景，但忽视了金融特定风险。金融LLM面临合规违规、欺诈促进和系统性信任侵蚀等问题，需要针对性评估。我们引入了FinRED，这是一个由金融专家开发的专家指导的金融LLM安全评估红队框架。FinRED使用一种新颖的两级分类法，将全球标准（如FATF和EU DORA）映射到从合规规避到复杂欺诈的威胁，结合一个可扩展的管道，通过专家定义的模式将真实金融文档转换为上下文丰富的红队行为提示（种子）。严格的专家验证确认了种子的合理性和现实性，以便进行有意义的LLM安全评估。我们还提供了一个经过专家验证的金融特定评分标准，超越了免责声明检查，更加贴近人类专家，而不是静态的一刀切评分标准，并将关键假阴性从28减少到12。FinRED与国际公认的风险管理和信息安全标准（如ISO/IEC 27001）保持一致，已在韩国金融安全研究所（FSI）的监管沙箱中部署，用于真实金融服务中的生成AI安全评估。为了减轻双重用途风险，数据集、生成管道、提示模板和评估框架对合格研究人员进行了限制，网址为https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper和https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED。

BreakFun: Jailbreaking LLMs via Object Instantiation under Simulated Code Execution
Authors: Amirkia Rafiei Oskooei, Mehmet S. Aktas
First: 2025-10-19T11:27:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-18T18:53:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted to AACL-IJCNLP 2026 Findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used because they process structures, syntax and code well, but this same ability also makes them paradoxically vulnerable. We introduce BreakFun, a jailbreak method that frames a harmful request as code-execution simulation. The prompt gives the model a benign Python class definition, the "Trojan Schema", and asks what that code would print if it ran. To answer, the model must create an object from the class and invent a value for each field, and the adversarial field names steer those values toward the attacker's goal. The harmful content therefore appears as a side-effect of the simulated instantiation, not as a direct answer. A three-part prompt carries the attack: an innocent frame, the Trojan Schema, and a Chain-of-Thought distraction. On JailbreakBench, BreakFun reaches an average attack success rate of 89% across 13 open-weight and commercial models (open-weight ~98%, API systems ~78%), and 100% on several. An ablation identifies the Trojan Schema as the strongest contributing component. As a counter-ablation probe we introduce Adversarial Prompt Deconstruction (APD), in which a second LLM writes out all readable text in the prompt before it judges safety. APD improves detection across three model families, and a probe-specific ablation shows that the transcription step gives most of the gain. Our findings show that LLM safety must account for content that a simulation task produces as a side-effect, not only for content that a user requests directly.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BreakFun：通过模拟代码执行中的对象实例化破解LLM
大型语言模型（LLM）因其良好的结构、语法和代码处理能力而被广泛使用，但这种能力也使其在某种程度上变得脆弱。我们介绍了BreakFun，这是一种将有害请求框架化为代码执行模拟的越狱方法。提示给模型一个无害的Python类定义，即“特洛伊模式”，并询问如果运行该代码会打印什么。为了回答，模型必须从类中创建一个对象并为每个字段发明一个值，而对抗性字段名称则将这些值引导至攻击者的目标。因此，有害内容作为模拟实例化的副作用出现，而不是直接答案。攻击通过三部分提示进行：一个无辜的框架、特洛伊模式和一个思维链干扰。在JailbreakBench上，BreakFun在13个开放权重和商业模型中达到了89%的平均攻击成功率（开放权重约98%，API系统约78%），并在多个模型上达到了100%。消融实验确定特洛伊模式是最强的贡献成分。作为对抗消融探针，我们引入了对抗性提示解构（APD），其中第二个LLM在判断安全性之前写出提示中的所有可读文本。APD提高了三个模型家族的检测能力，而特定探针的消融显示转录步骤带来了大部分增益。我们的研究结果表明，LLM安全性必须考虑模拟任务作为副作用产生的内容，而不仅仅是用户直接请求的内容。

This approach is well-motivated as it exploits the inherent processing capabilities of LLMs while circumventing traditional safety mechanisms. Local Sparsity Enables Unsupervised LLM Safety Detection
Authors: Xin Chen, Gil Kur, Alexander Shevchenko, Andreas Krause
First: 2026-09-17T12:23:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-17T12:23:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deployment-time safety methods for large language models (LLMs) are predominantly supervised and assume access to unsafe training data. Nevertheless, new attacks and harm categories regularly arise, not captured by models trained in such a supervised fashion. An alternative approach is to view this problem through the lens of anomaly detection, namely, to rely solely on modeling safe data and flagging out-of-distribution inputs. However, LLM activations lie in a high-dimensional space, raising concerns about whether anomaly detection is statistically feasible. We show that, under the linear representation hypothesis (LRH), there may indeed be hope. In the LRH concept space, which is typically recovered via a sparse autoencoder (SAE), nearby points share a small common active support. Using this local sparsity insight, we propose a framework for locally masked SAE-based anomaly detection, supported by theoretical justifications. We validate it on various architectures and datasets, including both capability-testing datasets and safety-specific datasets. Finally, when we allow algorithms to use 1% out-of-distribution data for calibration, locally sparse methods achieve near-optimal performance, demonstrating their ability to capture meaningful safety information while using only 1-2% of SAE neurons for computation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：局部稀疏性使无监督LLM安全检测成为可能
大型语言模型（LLMs）的部署时安全方法主要是监督性的，并假设可以访问不安全的训练数据。然而，新攻击和伤害类别定期出现，这些在以这种监督方式训练的模型中并未被捕获。另一种方法是通过异常检测的视角来看待这个问题，即仅依赖于建模安全数据并标记分布外输入。然而，LLM激活位于高维空间，这引发了关于异常检测在统计上是否可行的担忧。我们表明，在线性表示假设（LRH）下，确实可能存在希望。在LRH概念空间中，通常通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）恢复，附近点共享一个小的共同活跃支持。利用这一局部稀疏性洞察，我们提出了一种基于局部掩蔽SAE的异常检测框架，并得到了理论支持。我们在各种架构和数据集上进行了验证，包括能力测试数据集和安全特定数据集。最后，当我们允许算法使用1%的分布外数据进行校准时，局部稀疏方法实现了近乎最佳的性能，展示了它们在仅使用1-2%的SAE神经元进行计算时捕获有意义安全信息的能力。

From Intent to Action: Benchmarking LLM Safety in Vehicle Voice Command Authorization
Authors: Diba Afroze, Xingli Zhang, Yazhou Tu, Xiali Hei
First: 2026-09-17T03:20:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-17T03:20:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into vehicle voice assistants. But linking natural-language requests to vehicle functions creates a safety-critical authorization problem. Before executing a command, the system must choose whether to execute, refuse, clarify, require confirmation, defer to manual control, trigger an emergency response, or make no tool call. To our knowledge, prior evaluations do not isolate this pre-action decision across speaker role, authentication status, vehicle state, and tool availability. We introduce a 202-scenario benchmark with Reference Decisions under a seven-class taxonomy. We evaluate two local open-weight models and three API-based LLMs using Decision Alignment and safety-specific error metrics. Alignment ranges from 40.1% for Llama 3.2 3B to 89.1% for Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview. The API-based models score between 83.2% and 89.1%, with no statistically significant differences among them. Even these models produce two to three False Executes among 161 non-execution scenarios, and persistent errors remain in confirmation and manual-control decisions. A controlled Llama 3.2 3B ablation increases alignment to 40.1% under the structured authorization policy, versus 28.2-29.2% under schema-only and generic-safety baselines, but it does not eliminate False Executes. Structured LLM decisions are therefore insufficient as a standalone safety mechanism, and deployment requires an independent enforcement layer that verifies tool permissions and vehicle-state constraints before invoking any vehicle function.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从意图到行动：车辆语音命令授权中的大型语言模型安全性基准测试

HoloAegis: Frozen Representation, Topological Inference --- Minimally Parametric Safety Manifolds and Their Capability Boundaries for LLM Guardrails
Authors: Tak Ho Alex Li, Kaijie Liu, Lik-Hang Lee, Kin Chung Ho, Ping Shum, Michael K. Ng
First: 2026-08-09T05:05:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-15T12:22:48+00:00
Comments: Preprint v2, September 2026. 4 figures, 12 tables. Corrected and substantially revised from v1 (arXiv:2608.08485v1)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM safety guardrails face a fundamental tension: fine-tuning distorts pre-trained representations while generative judges incur prohibitive inference costs. We ask a complementary question: how far can safety be achieved through pure geometric reasoning over frozen representations, and where does it fail? We present HoloAegis, a minimally parametric topological inference framework that decouples representation from reasoning: an un-fine-tuned encoder maps text to the unit sphere S^{d-1}, and all decisions reduce to Gibbs-Boltzmann free-energy differences over pre-computed anchor centroids. We contribute a boundary-mapping study rather than a leaderboard claim. On a frozen three-benchmark protocol, HoloAegis (3.2 MB) statistically matches WildGuard-7B (14 GB) on toxicity (0.96 vs. 0.96), exceeds it on harmful behaviors (0.99 vs. 0.79), and cedes oversafety detection (0.62 vs. 0.98) -- while ShieldGemma-2B fails on indirect harms (0.34). These failure modes are complementary and mechanistically traceable: potential-difference scoring senses manifold clustering, whereas policy-conditioned LLM judging requires explicit taxonomy matching. We restate our Topological Boundary Stability conjecture in ratio form and validate it via reference-set bootstrap: anchor banks reduce score variance 4-15x and boundary displacement to approximately 0.44 + 0.23 sqrt(k/K) of the full-space estimator. Per-domain analysis further reveals that geometric separability tracks within-domain semantic homogeneity. Our results chart where geometric guardrails substitute for, and where they must defer to, LLM judges.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HoloAegis：冻结表示、拓扑推理 --- 最小参数安全流形及其对LLM保护措施的能力边界
当前LLM安全保护措施面临根本性矛盾：微调扭曲了预训练表示，而生成性判断则产生了高昂的推理成本。我们提出一个互补性问题：通过对冻结表示的纯几何推理，安全性可以达到多远，在哪里又会失败？我们提出HoloAegis，一个最小参数的拓扑推理框架，解耦表示与推理：未微调的编码器将文本映射到单位球面S^{d-1}，所有决策归结为对预计算锚点质心的Gibbs-Boltzmann自由能差异。我们贡献了一个边界映射研究，而不是排行榜声明。在一个冻结的三基准协议上，HoloAegis（3.2 MB）在毒性上与WildGuard-7B（14 GB）统计匹配（0.96 vs. 0.96），在有害行为上超越它（0.99 vs. 0.79），而在过度安全检测上则让步（0.62 vs. 0.98）——同时ShieldGemma-2B在间接危害上失败（0.34）。这些失败模式是互补的，并且在机制上可追溯：电位差评分感知流形聚类，而政策条件的LLM判断则需要明确的分类匹配。我们以比率形式重述我们的拓扑边界稳定性猜想，并通过参考集自助法进行验证：锚库将得分方差降低4-15倍，边界位移约为全空间估计器的0.44 + 0.23 sqrt(k/K)。每个领域的分析进一步揭示几何可分性与领域内语义同质性之间的关系。我们的结果描绘了几何保护措施替代和必须依赖LLM判断的地方。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of ensuring safety in large language models (LLMs), which traditionally relies on fine-tuning that can distort pre-trained representations or on generative judges that incur high inference costs. The proposed method, HoloAegis, introduces a minimally parametric topological inference framework that utilizes frozen representations, mapping text to a unit sphere and making decisions based on Gibbs-Boltzmann free-energy differences over pre-computed anchor centroids. This approach is motivated by the need to explore the limits of safety achievable through geometric reasoning alone. The contributions include a boundary-mapping study that demonstrates HoloAegis statistically matches the performance of a larger model on toxicity and exceeds it on harmful behaviors, while also revealing complementary failure modes in safety detection. The methodology shows that anchor banks significantly reduce score variance and boundary displacement, providing insights into the relationship between geometric separability and semantic homogeneity within domains.
本文探讨了当前大型语言模型（LLM）安全防护措施面临的挑战，特别是微调扭曲预训练表示和生成判断器的高推理成本等问题。现有方法通常依赖于微调或复杂的判断机制，这可能导致低效和不准确。提出的HoloAegis框架引入了一种最小参数拓扑推理方法，利用冻结表示，允许在不需要微调的情况下进行几何推理。该方法通过在有害行为检测上超越更大模型的表现，同时在毒性检测上与之统计匹配，显著贡献了几何推理在安全评估中的有效性。该方法论涉及将文本映射到单位球体，并基于Gibbs-Boltzmann自由能差异做出决策，在冻结的三基准协议上取得了竞争性结果，支持了以降低计算成本增强LLM安全性的目标。
Useless but Safe? Benchmarking Utility Recovery with User Intent Clarification in Multi-Turn Conversations
Authors: Mingqian Zheng, Malia Morgan, Liwei Jiang, Carolyn Rose, Maarten Sap
First: 2026-04-29T18:37:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-12T19:48:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM safety alignment techniques improve model robustness against adversarial attacks, but overlook whether and how LLMs can recover helpfulness when benign users clarify their intent. We introduce CarryOnBench, the first interactive benchmark that measures whether LLMs can revise their interpretation of user intent and recover utility, while remaining safe through multi-turn conversations. Starting from 398 seemingly harmful queries with benign underlying intents, we simulate 5,970 conversations by varying user follow-up sequences, evaluating 14 models on both intent-aligned utility and safety. CarryOnBench yields 1,866 different conversation flows of 4--12 turns, totaling 23,880 model responses. We design Ben-Util, a checklist-based metric that evaluates how well each model response fulfills the user's benign information need using atomic items. At turn one, models fulfill only 10.5--37.6% of the user's benign information need. When the same query includes the benign intent upfront, models fulfill 25.1--72.1%, confirming that models withhold information due to intent misinterpretation, not limited knowledge. With benign clarifications in multi-turn conversations, 13 of 14 models approach or exceed this single-turn baseline, yet recovery cost varies across models. We identify two failure modes invisible to single-turn evaluations---unsafe recovery, where a model updates at disproportionate safety cost and redundant recovery, where a model recycles prior responses rather than providing new information. Moreover, conversations converge to similar harmfulness levels regardless of how conservative the model starts. These findings expose a gap that single-turn evaluations miss---whether a model is appropriately cautious or simply unresponsive to clarified user intent.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无用但安全？在多轮对话中通过用户意图澄清基准测试实用性恢复
当前的LLM安全对齐技术提高了模型对抗攻击的鲁棒性，但忽视了LLM在良性用户澄清意图时是否以及如何恢复有用性。我们引入CarryOnBench，这是第一个交互式基准，测量LLM是否能够修正对用户意图的解释并恢复实用性，同时在多轮对话中保持安全。从398个看似有害的查询开始，这些查询具有良性的潜在意图，我们通过变化用户后续序列模拟了5,970个对话，评估了14个模型在意图对齐的实用性和安全性方面的表现。CarryOnBench产生了1,866种不同的对话流程，轮次为4到12，总计23,880个模型响应。我们设计了Ben-Util，这是一个基于清单的指标，评估每个模型响应在多大程度上满足用户的良性信息需求。在第一轮中，模型仅满足用户良性信息需求的10.5%到37.6%。当同一查询前置良性意图时，模型满足25.1%到72.1%，确认模型因意图误解而隐瞒信息，而非知识有限。在多轮对话中，13个模型接近或超过这一单轮基线，但恢复成本在模型之间有所不同。我们识别出两种在单轮评估中不可见的失败模式——不安全恢复，即模型以不成比例的安全成本进行更新，以及冗余恢复，即模型重复之前的响应而不是提供新信息。此外，无论模型起始多么保守，对话都趋向于相似的有害性水平。这些发现揭示了单轮评估所忽视的一个差距——模型是否适当地谨慎，或仅仅对澄清的用户意图无反应。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of current large language model (LLM) safety alignment techniques, which focus on robustness against adversarial attacks but fail to assess the models' ability to recover helpfulness when users clarify their intent in multi-turn conversations. Previous methods primarily evaluated models in single-turn contexts, neglecting the dynamics of ongoing interactions, leading to misinterpretations of user intent and inadequate responses. The proposed approach, CarryOnBench, introduces an interactive benchmark that simulates 5,970 conversations to evaluate 14 models on their ability to align with user intent while maintaining safety. The study reveals that at the first turn, models fulfill only 10.5–37.6% of benign user needs, but with clarifications, 13 out of 14 models improve their performance, although recovery costs vary. This research contributes to understanding the shortcomings of single-turn evaluations and highlights the importance of assessing model responses in multi-turn scenarios, revealing failure modes such as unsafe recovery and redundant recovery that were previously unrecognized.
本研究解决了当前大型语言模型（LLM）安全对齐技术的局限性，这些技术专注于抵御对抗性攻击，但未能评估LLM在用户澄清意图时如何恢复有用性。以往的方法主要在单轮交互中评估模型，忽视了多轮对话的动态性及模型可能误解用户意图的潜力。提出的方法CarryOnBench引入了一种交互式基准，评估LLM在保持安全的同时，修正对用户意图的理解并恢复效用的能力。该研究基于398个具有良性意图的有害查询模拟了5970个对话，使用新颖的检查表指标Ben-Util评估14个模型。结果表明，模型最初只满足用户需求的一小部分，但在澄清后，许多模型的表现有所改善，尽管恢复成本各异。这些发现揭示了单轮评估所忽视的关键失败模式，强调了对模型响应用户意图的更全面评估的必要性。
Adaptive Adversaries: A Multi-Turn, Multi-LLM Benchmark for LLM Agent Security
Authors: Devina Jain, David Hartmann, Chuan Li
First: 2026-07-20T15:30:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-11T18:06:53+00:00
Comments: Second Workshop on Agents in the Wild: Safety, Security, and Beyond
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based agents process external content, exposing them to prompt injection and multi-turn manipulation. We present a 21-scenario benchmark for adaptive cross-session attacks against fresh-session LLM defenders: an autonomous LLM attacker observes prior defender responses and pivots across rounds, while each defender response is evaluated as a fresh interaction. A controlled 3 x 3 attacker-defender matrix contains 945 battles. Restricting scoring to the first round yields 0-1% attack success rate (ASR); allowing 15 rounds yields 7.9-16.8%. Pooling three attacker LLMs uncovers 1.7-2.2 times as many unique successful inputs as the best single attacker, at three times the battle budget. Aggregate rates conceal opposing scenario-specific weaknesses in session-secret protection and authority handling, preserved in two higher-sample evaluations. On six scenarios, adding one provenance paragraph reduces ASR from 110/270 to 70/270, with selective effects across tasks. History and defender-state controls, together with frozen replay, characterize how the interaction protocol changes the result. A competition adds 18,422 held-out battles on a fixed gpt-oss-20b backbone and complementary benign-task evaluations. The benchmark exposes attacker and defender models, harnesses, scenarios, session state, and interaction budgets as configurable choices for systematic security evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自适应对手：多回合、多LLM基准测试用于LLM代理安全性
基于LLM的代理处理外部内容，使其暴露于提示注入和多回合操控中。我们提出了一个21场景基准，用于针对新会话LLM防御者的自适应跨会话攻击：自主LLM攻击者观察先前防御者的响应并在回合间进行调整，而每个防御者的响应被评估为一次新的交互。一个受控的3 x 3攻击者-防御者矩阵包含945场战斗。将评分限制在第一回合的攻击成功率（ASR）为0-1%；允许15回合则为7.9-16.8%。汇聚三个攻击者LLM发现的独特成功输入数量是最佳单一攻击者的1.7-2.2倍，战斗预算是三倍。汇总率掩盖了会话秘密保护和权限处理中的相对场景特定弱点，这在两个更高样本评估中得以保留。在六个场景中，添加一个来源段落将ASR从110/270降低到70/270，任务间效果选择性。历史和防御者状态控制，以及冻结重放，表征了交互协议如何改变结果。一个竞赛增加了在固定gpt-oss-20b基础上和补充良性任务评估的18,422场保留战斗。该基准揭示了攻击者和防御者模型、工具、场景、会话状态和交互预算作为系统安全评估的可配置选择。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the vulnerabilities of LLM-based agents to prompt injection and multi-turn manipulation, highlighting the need for robust security measures in AI systems. Previous methods lacked comprehensive benchmarks for evaluating adaptive attacks, leading to insufficient understanding of LLM defenders' weaknesses. This paper proposes a novel 21-scenario benchmark that simulates adaptive cross-session attacks, allowing attackers to learn from prior interactions while evaluating defenders in fresh contexts. The methodology includes a controlled 3 x 3 attacker-defender matrix with 945 battles, revealing that allowing multiple rounds significantly increases attack success rates. The findings demonstrate that pooling multiple attacker LLMs can yield a greater variety of successful inputs, while specific adjustments, such as adding provenance information, can notably reduce attack success rates in certain scenarios, thus providing a structured approach to evaluating LLM security.
本研究针对基于LLM的代理在提示注入和多轮操控方面的脆弱性，这可能会危及其安全性。以往的方法缺乏全面的评估框架来评估LLM防御者对自适应攻击的有效性，导致对其弱点的理解不足。所提出的方法引入了一个21场景的基准，用于自适应跨会话攻击，从而允许对LLM防御者在多次交互中的反应进行更深入的检查。该方法包括一个受控的攻击者-防御者矩阵，包含945场战斗，结果显示允许多轮交互显著提高了攻击成功率。研究发现，汇聚多个攻击者LLM可以产生更多独特的成功输入，突显了LLM系统中会话秘密保护和权威处理的改进需求。总体而言，本文为评估LLM安全性贡献了一个系统框架，揭示了关键弱点，并为增强防御者策略提供了见解。
'Ghaib in Translation' aka Unseen Harm: Measuring Cross-Script Safety Inconsistency with 'Missed-in-Urdu' Scores in LLM Hate Speech Detection
Authors: Fawzia Zehra, Kara-Isitt, Sonal Khosla, Stephen Swift
First: 2026-08-25T07:58:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-09T16:54:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Urdu, the world's tenth most spoken language with 246 million speakers, remains almost entirely absent from mainstream LLM safety evaluation and nine years of WOAH proceedings. To investigate whether this absence has measurable consequences for content moderation reliability, five large language models, GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen-2.5, and Llama-3.1, were tested across six datasets spanning Nastaliq Urdu, Roman Urdu, English, and code-switched Urdu-English. Across the five Urdu-script datasets, label instability between original-script and English-translation classification ranged from 15.9% (Gemini 2.5 Flash) to 31.6% (Qwen-2.5), with a 'Missed-in-Urdu' rate, content flagged as harmful in English translation but passed as normal in the original script, ranging from 2.4% to 9.9% (median 4.3%). A complete enumeration of all 205 papers across nine ALW/WOAH editions via the ACL Anthology API confirms zero dedicated Urdu papers across the entire period. Results indicate that current LLMs provide uneven safety assurance across Urdu's script varieties, with smaller open-weight models showing substantially higher instability and missed-harm rates than frontier closed models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：'翻译中的隐秘'即未见的危害：使用'乌尔都语遗漏'分数测量跨脚本安全不一致性在LLM仇恨言论检测中的应用
乌尔都语是全球第十大语言，拥有2.46亿说话者，但在主流LLM安全评估和九年的WOAH会议中几乎完全缺席。为了调查这种缺席是否对内容审核的可靠性产生可测量的影响，测试了五个大型语言模型：GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen-2.5和Llama-3.1，涵盖了六个数据集，包括Nastaliq乌尔都语、罗马乌尔都语、英语和代码切换的乌尔都英语。在五个乌尔都脚本数据集中，原始脚本与英语翻译分类之间的标签不稳定性范围为15.9%（Gemini 2.5 Flash）至31.6%（Qwen-2.5），'乌尔都语遗漏'率，即在英语翻译中标记为有害但在原始脚本中通过的内容，范围为2.4%至9.9%（中位数4.3%）。通过ACL Anthology API对九个ALW/WOAH版本中的205篇论文进行的完整枚举确认，在整个期间内没有专门的乌尔都语论文。结果表明，当前的LLM在乌尔都语的脚本变体之间提供的不均匀安全保障，较小的开放权重模型显示出明显更高的不稳定性和遗漏危害率，超过了前沿的封闭模型。
Summary / 总结
This article addresses the significant gap in the evaluation of large language models (LLMs) regarding their safety in Urdu, a language with 246 million speakers that has been overlooked in mainstream assessments. Previous methods have failed to adequately evaluate the performance of LLMs in different scripts, leading to inconsistencies in content moderation. The proposed approach investigates the reliability of LLMs by measuring cross-script safety inconsistencies using 'Missed-in-Urdu' scores across various datasets. The study reveals that label instability between original Urdu scripts and English translations can be as high as 31.6%, with a notable 'Missed-in-Urdu' rate indicating harmful content that is overlooked in the original script. The findings highlight the uneven safety assurance provided by current LLMs across different Urdu script varieties, emphasizing the need for more comprehensive evaluations in future research.
本研究关注乌尔都语这一广泛使用语言在大型语言模型（LLMs）安全评估中的显著缺口，强调了对内容审核可靠性可能造成的后果。以往的方法在很大程度上忽视了乌尔都语，导致不同书写系统之间的仇恨言论检测不一致，尤其是在原始乌尔都语与英语翻译之间。所提出的方法系统地评估了五个LLMs在多个乌尔都语数据集上的表现，揭示了显著的标签不稳定性和明显的“乌尔都语遗漏”率，表明原始书写系统中存在未被检测的有害内容。该研究有助于理解LLMs中的跨书写系统安全不一致性，并强调了需要包容性评估框架。该方法论涉及在六个数据集上测试LLMs，表明当前模型在安全保障方面存在不均衡，小型模型的遗漏有害内容的比率更高，从而支持在多语言环境中改进评估实践的必要性。
Style Over Substance: Content-Invariant Wrappers Flip LLM Safety-Judge Verdicts
Authors: Yongxi Zhou, Wenbo Ye, Yuanzhe Liu, Zihan Dong, Junwei Yao
First: 2026-09-08T04:27:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-08T04:27:22+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 1 table. Code, wrappers, and per-verdict labels: https://github.com/Yongxi-Zhou/safety-judge-robustness
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Automatic safety judges -- systems such as Llama Guard or a GPT-4o grading prompt that decide whether a model's reply is harmful -- produce the numbers behind almost every reported jailbreak success rate, defense evaluation, and safety leaderboard. We ask whether these judges grade what a reply contains or how it sounds. We keep a reply's content fixed and add content-invariant style wrappers: fixed strings placed before or after the reply that change only its tone (an educational disclaimer, a fake safety "reasoning" block, a token refusal followed by the unchanged harmful body), or, on harmless refusals, framing that merely sounds dangerous. The body is preserved byte-for-byte, so a faithful judge must return the same verdict, and any flip is an error of the judge, not a change in safety. Over 600 JailbreakBench replies x up to 7 forms x 8 judges, we measure flip rates with paired significance tests and measured noise floors. Findings are precise rather than universal: most judges barely move, but specific judges harbor cheaply exploitable blind spots. A token-refusal wrapper flips 19.9% of GPT-4o-mini's correct "unsafe" verdicts (noise floor 0.5%; 18.2% under majority-of-three re-scoring) yet moves Claude only 0.4%. The deployed Llama Guard 4 is deterministically gamed: an "educational course" framing flips 12.3% of its harmful verdicts to safe. A second deployed guard (gpt-oss-safeguard-20b) is immune, and rewriting only the grading prompt (StrongREJECT-style) cuts the attack tenfold on the identical model -- the vulnerability lives in the judge, not the content. A two-annotator human validation confirms 100% content invariance and 90% of flips as judge errors (kappa 0.95-1.0), and a bootstrap shows the underlying model ranking is already unstable to sampling alone. We release the dataset, wrappers, code, and per-verdict labels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：形式胜于内容：内容不变的包装翻转LLM安全评判结果
自动安全评判系统——如Llama Guard或GPT-4o评分提示，决定模型回复是否有害——生成几乎所有报告的越狱成功率、防御评估和安全排行榜背后的数据。我们探讨这些评判是根据回复的内容还是其表述方式进行评分。我们保持回复内容不变，添加内容不变的风格包装：在回复前或后放置的固定字符串，仅改变其语气（教育免责声明、虚假的安全“推理”块、后接不变的有害内容的拒绝令），或者在无害拒绝的情况下，仅仅听起来危险的框架。主体逐字保留，因此一个忠实的评判必须返回相同的判决，任何翻转都是评判的错误，而不是安全性的变化。在超过600个JailbreakBench回复中，乘以最多7种形式和8个评判，我们通过配对显著性测试和测量噪声底线来测量翻转率。发现是精确的而非普遍的：大多数评判几乎没有变化，但特定评判存在廉价可利用的盲点。一个令牌拒绝包装翻转了19.9%的GPT-4o-mini的正确“危险”判决（噪声底线0.5%；在三人多数重新评分下为18.2%），但对Claude的影响仅为0.4%。已部署的Llama Guard 4被确定性地操控：“教育课程”框架将12.3%的有害判决翻转为安全。第二个已部署的防护（gpt-oss-safeguard-20b）是免疫的，仅重写评分提示（StrongREJECT风格）就将攻击减少十倍，脆弱性存在于评判中，而非内容。两位注释员的人类验证确认100%的内容不变性和90%的翻转为评判错误（kappa 0.95-1.0），而自助法显示基础模型排名已经对抽样不稳定。我们发布数据集、包装、代码和每个判决标签。
Summary / 总结
This research investigates the effectiveness of automatic safety judges, which evaluate the harmfulness of model replies, by examining whether their assessments are influenced by content or merely by presentation style. Previous methods have relied on fixed content evaluations, leading to vulnerabilities that can be exploited through superficial changes in tone or framing. The proposed approach introduces content-invariant style wrappers that alter the presentation without changing the underlying content, thereby revealing flaws in the judges' assessments. The study employs a rigorous methodology involving over 600 replies tested against multiple judges, revealing that while most judges remain stable, specific ones exhibit exploitable weaknesses. Notably, a token-refusal wrapper was found to flip nearly 20% of GPT-4o-mini's unsafe verdicts, highlighting significant vulnerabilities in certain safety judges. The paper contributes to understanding the limitations of current safety evaluation systems and provides tools for further research by releasing datasets and code.
本研究调查了自动安全评估系统的可靠性，这些系统评估语言模型的响应是否有害，重点分析评估是否受到响应内容或风格的影响。以往的方法主要集中在内容分析上，但往往忽视了风格变化如何操控评估者的裁决，从而导致安全评估的脆弱性。提出的方法引入了内容不变的风格包装，这些包装在不改变内容的情况下改变响应的语气，从而清晰地评估评估者的表现。该研究采用了一种全面的方法，涉及对600多个响应进行测试，以测量这些包装翻转安全判断的比率。研究结果表明，尽管大多数评估者保持稳定，但某些评估者表现出显著的盲点，其中一个包装导致GPT-4o-mini的不安全裁决翻转率达到19.9%，表明问题出在评估者而非内容本身。该研究有助于理解当前安全评估系统的局限性，并提供改进其稳健性的工具。
The Oversight Gap: What LLM Safety Monitors Miss, and Why It Is Not Capability
Authors: Xin Xu
First: 2026-09-07T07:57:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-07T07:57:28+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 3 figures. Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Several properties safety monitors are asked to certify, among them cross-tenant noninterference, sandbagging and evaluation awareness, are 2-safety hyperproperties, witnessed only by two executions. The standard consequence is a binary impossibility: one trace cannot decide them. We replace the binary with a measurement. A tight bound puts the balanced accuracy of any single-trace monitor at $\tfrac12+\tfrac12\,TV(P_0,P_1)$, turning undecidability into a graded detectability frontier and defining an oversight gap: a monitor's shortfall below it. On a leak family with closed-form $TV$, nine LLM monitors are optimal at $TV=0$ but capture little signal as $TV$ grows; at $TV=1$, where a 20-line membership check scores $100\%$, they average $60.9\%$. That shortfall is mostly not capability: naming what to check closes $61\%$ of it while leaving the $TV=0$ control at chance. The same split runs through a $2{\times}2$ factorial: an imagined second run leaves monitors at chance ($50.4\%$) while the same rule on an executed second run reaches $90.0\%$, and a stored oracle without a comparison procedure yields only $68.2\%$. Information and procedure are each necessary and neither is capability. Under nondeterminism, replay tracks a closed-form $k$-replay curve only under the right projection, and a projection frontier shows the resulting dilemma is forced: narrow misses $98.6\%$ of off-channel leaks, broad flags $75.7\%$ of clean traffic, and attainable accuracy decays like $1/(qm)$ in the benign-variation rate and the channel count. Finally, two frontier LLM judges certified an earlier version of our own benchmark as sound while a sign test found a directional bias ($p=2.7\times10^{-5}$) that invalidated three of our findings. Construction validity for hyperproperty benchmarks should be proved mechanically, not audited by models.
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of existing safety monitors for large language models (LLMs), which struggle to certify complex properties like cross-tenant noninterference and evaluation awareness due to their binary nature. Previous methods have been unable to effectively measure these properties, leading to a binary impossibility in decision-making. The proposed approach introduces a graded detectability frontier that quantifies monitor performance, revealing an oversight gap where monitors fall short of optimal detection capabilities. The methodology involves analyzing the balanced accuracy of various LLM monitors against a leak family characterized by total variation distance (TV). The findings indicate that while monitors perform optimally at TV=0, their effectiveness declines as TV increases, with an average accuracy of 60.9% at TV=1, highlighting that the shortfall is not solely due to capability but also due to the lack of specific checks. This paper contributes to the understanding of LLM safety by establishing a framework for measuring monitor performance and demonstrating the necessity of both information and procedure in achieving reliable safety assessments.
本研究解决了现有大型语言模型（LLM）安全监控器的局限性，特别是它们无法认证某些复杂属性，如跨租户非干扰和沙袋效应，这些属性被归类为2-安全超属性。传统方法在决策中面临二元不可能性，导致监控效果不佳。所提出的方法引入了一个分级可检测性前沿，允许对监督缺口进行测量，而不是二元评估，从而提供了对监控性能的更细致理解。本文的贡献在于建立了一个量化监控器短缺的框架，并表明大多数性能问题并非由于能力不足，而是由于所使用的信息和程序。该方法论涉及分析不同场景下各种LLM监控器的平衡准确性，揭示了尽管一些监控器在低总变差（TV）下表现最佳，但随着TV的增加，其有效性下降，某些条件下的平均准确率为60.9%，表明所提出的方法可以显著增强安全监控任务的理解和性能。
SAEs Can Improve Unlearning: Dynamic Sparse Autoencoder Guardrails for Precision Unlearning in LLMs
Authors: Aashiq Muhamed, Jacopo Bonato, Mona Diab, Virginia Smith
First: 2025-04-11T01:24:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-07T03:32:59+00:00
Comments: COLM 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine unlearning is a promising approach to improve LLM safety by removing unwanted knowledge from the model. However, prevailing gradient-based unlearning methods suffer from issues such as high computational costs, hyperparameter instability, poor sequential unlearning capability, vulnerability to relearning attacks, low data efficiency, and lack of interpretability. While Sparse Autoencoders are well-suited to improve these aspects by enabling targeted activation-based unlearning, prior approaches underperform gradient-based methods. This work demonstrates that, contrary to these earlier findings, SAEs can significantly improve unlearning when employed dynamically. We introduce $\textbf{Dynamic DAE Guardrails}$ (DSG), a novel method for precision unlearning that leverages principled feature selection and a dynamic classifier. Our experiments show DSG substantially outperforms leading unlearning methods, achieving superior forget-utility trade-offs. DSG addresses key drawbacks of gradient-based approaches for unlearning -- offering enhanced computational efficiency and stability, robust performance in sequential unlearning, stronger resistance to relearning attacks, better data efficiency including zero-shot settings, and more interpretable unlearning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAEs可以改善遗忘：用于LLMs精确遗忘的动态稀疏自编码器护栏
机器遗忘是一种有前景的方法，通过从模型中移除不需要的知识来提高LLM的安全性。然而，现有的基于梯度的遗忘方法存在高计算成本、超参数不稳定、顺序遗忘能力差、易受重学习攻击、数据效率低和缺乏可解释性等问题。稀疏自编码器非常适合通过启用针对性激活的遗忘来改善这些方面，但先前的方法在性能上不及基于梯度的方法。本研究表明，与早期发现相反，SAEs在动态使用时可以显著改善遗忘。我们引入了$\textbf{动态DAE护栏}$（DSG），这是一种利用原则性特征选择和动态分类器的精确遗忘新方法。我们的实验表明，DSG在遗忘方法中显著优于领先的遗忘方法，实现了更好的遗忘-效用权衡。DSG解决了基于梯度的遗忘方法的关键缺陷——提供了更高的计算效率和稳定性、在顺序遗忘中的强大性能、对重学习攻击的更强抵抗力、更好的数据效率（包括零样本设置）以及更可解释的遗忘。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the challenges associated with machine unlearning in large language models (LLMs), particularly the limitations of existing gradient-based methods, which include high computational costs, instability, and vulnerability to relearning attacks. The proposed approach, Dynamic DAE Guardrails (DSG), distinguishes itself by utilizing dynamic sparse autoencoders to enhance targeted activation-based unlearning, thereby improving computational efficiency, stability, and interpretability. The contribution of this paper lies in demonstrating that DSG significantly outperforms traditional unlearning methods, achieving better forget-utility trade-offs and addressing the critical issues of sequential unlearning and data efficiency. Through rigorous experimentation, the authors validate that DSG not only enhances performance but also meets the goals of effective and precise unlearning in LLMs.

Behind Harmful Compliance: Behavioral and Mechanistic Divergence Across LLM Jailbreaks
Authors: Md Rysul Kabir, Zoran Tiganj
First: 2026-04-20T17:01:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-05T18:10:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open-weight language models can be rendered unsafe through several parameter-level interventions, yet models with matched harmful compliance can exhibit fundamentally different failure modes. We compare harmful supervised fine-tuning (SFT), harmful reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), and refusal-feature abliteration in Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B using harmfulness, capability, self-audit, safety reflection, representation similarity, and refusal-direction repair. All three routes reach near-ceiling harmfulness, but SFT causes the broadest capability loss and representational drift; abliteration yields localized, family-dependent refusal-feature suppression; and RLVR largely preserves base-model capability, explicit safety judgments, and representation geometry while retargeting behavior toward compliance. RLVR models consequently remain unusually responsive to safety-reflection prompts despite complying under direct prompting. We further find that harmful RLVR induces capability-blind compliance: models claim to complete unavailable actions and fabricate information about nonexistent entities. Targeted RLVR calibration substantially reduces this false acceptance without restoring safety or degrading general capability. These results show that harmful compliance, harm recognition, and capability awareness are separable behavioral axes, and that self-audit and hallucination patterns are not necessarily robust safety signals under adaptive post-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：有害合规背后的行为和机制差异：LLM越狱的比较
开放权重语言模型可以通过多种参数级干预变得不安全，但具有匹配有害合规的模型可能表现出根本不同的失败模式。我们比较了Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.1-8B中的有害监督微调（SFT）、有害可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）和拒绝特征消融，使用有害性、能力、自我审计、安全反思、表示相似性和拒绝方向修复。所有三种途径都接近极限有害性，但SFT导致最广泛的能力损失和表示漂移；消融产生局部的、依赖于家族的拒绝特征抑制；而RLVR在重新定向合规行为的同时，基本上保留了基础模型的能力、明确的安全判断和表示几何。因此，尽管在直接提示下合规，RLVR模型仍对安全反思提示表现出异常的响应。我们进一步发现，有害的RLVR会导致能力盲目的合规：模型声称完成不可用的动作，并虚构关于不存在实体的信息。针对性的RLVR校准显著减少了这种错误接受，而不恢复安全或降低一般能力。这些结果表明，有害合规、伤害识别和能力意识是可分离的行为轴，自我审计和幻觉模式在适应性后训练下不一定是稳健的安全信号。
Summary / 总结
This research investigates the safety vulnerabilities of open-weight language models, particularly how different parameter-level interventions can lead to harmful compliance with varying failure modes. Previous methods, including harmful supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and harmful reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), have shown limitations such as significant capability loss and representational drift. The proposed approach, RLVR, addresses these issues by preserving model capabilities and enhancing safety judgments while retargeting behavior towards compliance. The paper contributes to understanding the distinct behavioral axes of harmful compliance and capability awareness, employing a comparative analysis of SFT, RLVR, and refusal-feature abliteration on models like Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B. The findings indicate that while all methods achieve high harmfulness, RLVR maintains better overall performance and responsiveness to safety prompts, highlighting the complexity of safety signals in adaptive post-training scenarios.
本研究探讨了开放权重语言模型的安全漏洞，特别是不同参数级干预如何导致有害合规性及其不同的失败模式。以往的方法，包括有害的监督微调（SFT）和有害的强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR），显示出显著的能力损失和表征漂移等局限性。所提出的方法RLVR通过在重新定向行为以实现合规的同时保持基础模型能力，解决了这些问题，从而保持对安全反思提示的响应能力。本文的贡献在于理解有害合规性、伤害识别和能力意识的可分性，表明自我审计和幻觉模式并不是可靠的安全指标。该方法论涉及比较不同模型中的SFT、RLVR和拒绝特征消融，结果显示RLVR显著减少了对不可用行为的错误接受，同时保持一般能力，从而支持了研究目标。
TIER: Threat Implicitness Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Safety Behaviors
Authors: Thu-Hien Trinh-Thi, Hai-Yen Vong, Thanh-Ha Ung-Dung, Tram Ho
First: 2026-09-04T13:13:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-04T13:13:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM safety benchmarks largely rely on binary metrics, overlooking how models respond to harmful prompts with varying threat implicitness. We introduce TIER, a Threat Implicitness Benchmark for behavioral safety evaluation of LLMs. TIER covers four risk domains and four threat levels, from explicit harmful requests to sophisticated jailbreaks. Responses are assessed using a six-label behavior scale and two independent LLM judges. Experiments on six open-weight LLMs show that safety behaviors evolve gradually across threat levels rather than shifting directly from refusal to compliance. Contextual prompts yield the most diverse behaviors, while jailbreaks reveal the largest robustness gaps. Furthermore, models with similar Attack Success Rates can exhibit distinct response distributions, highlighting the need for behavior-aware LLM safety evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TIER：评估LLM安全行为的威胁隐含性基准
当前LLM安全基准主要依赖于二元指标，忽视了模型对不同威胁隐含性的有害提示的响应。我们引入TIER，一个用于LLM行为安全评估的威胁隐含性基准。TIER涵盖四个风险领域和四个威胁级别，从明确的有害请求到复杂的越狱。使用六级行为量表和两个独立的LLM评审对响应进行评估。对六个开放权重LLM的实验表明，安全行为在威胁级别之间逐渐演变，而不是直接从拒绝转变为遵从。上下文提示产生了最丰富的行为，而越狱则揭示了最大的鲁棒性差距。此外，具有相似攻击成功率的模型可能表现出不同的响应分布，突显了对行为意识的LLM安全评估的需求。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of current LLM safety benchmarks, which primarily use binary metrics and fail to capture the nuances of model responses to harmful prompts with varying levels of threat implicitness. Previous methods have not adequately evaluated the spectrum of safety behaviors, leading to a lack of understanding of model vulnerabilities. The proposed TIER benchmark offers a more comprehensive evaluation by categorizing threats into four risk domains and levels, utilizing a six-label behavior scale assessed by independent judges. The findings indicate that models with similar attack success rates can still demonstrate different response patterns, underscoring the importance of a behavior-aware approach to LLM safety evaluation.

IndicSafeEval: Safety Robustness of Large Language Models under Multilingual Persuasive Jailbreak Attacks
Authors: Saikat Mondal, Mamta, Deeksha Varshney, Oana Cocarascu, Asif Ekbal
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-09-03T12:54:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-04T11:46:25+00:00
Comments: 38 pages, 7 figures, 33 tables. Accepted to Findings of EMNLP 2026. Contains examples of harmful model outputs
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in multilingual settings, yet their safety is still evaluated primarily in English. This limits our understanding of how alignment failures manifest in low-resource and culturally diverse languages. We introduce IndicSafeEval, a persuasion-based jailbreak evaluation framework for Indian languages. Our benchmark combines ten safety critical content categories with six human-like persuasive strategies across four different Indian languages, such as Hindi, Bengali, Marathi and Punjabi, resulting in 7,200 adversarial prompts. We conduct a systematic black-box evaluation of several open-source LLMs to examine how their safety behaviour varies across languages, persuasion strategies, and risk categories. Our analysis shows that the model does not behave equally safely across all languages and prompt styles. Instead, safety performance depends strongly on both the languages used and the way a request is phrased using persuasive cues. We further observe that different risk categories exhibit different levels of vulnerability, with some types of harmful content being significantly more susceptible to persuasion-based jailbreaks than others. These findings reveal important limitations of current safety evaluations, which are largely English-centric, and underscore the need for multilingual and persuasion-aware benchmarking frameworks to more accurately assess real-world LLM safety. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/MonSaikat/IndicSafeEval. Warning: this paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.

FLY-EVAL++: An Evidence-Driven Evaluation Protocol for Safety-Constrained Flight Prediction with Large Language Models
Authors: Yalun Wu, Junfeng Fang, Jiawei Wang, Haotian Liu, Qijun Yang, Minghan Yang, Hongcheng Guo, Zhoujun Li, Boyang Wang
Venue: Proceedings of the Conference on Language Modeling (COLM), 2026
First: 2026-09-03T16:00:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-03T16:00:02+00:00
Comments: Published as a conference paper at COLM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical, physics-governed environments requires more than accuracy-based metrics, because predictions that are numerically close to the ground truth can still violate operational constraints, combine fields in physically inconsistent ways, or fail to produce usable structured outputs. Existing evaluation protocols do not measure these failure modes reliably. We propose FLY-EVAL++, an evidence-driven evaluation protocol that combines deterministic verification of protocol compliance, physical feasibility, and safety constraints with fixed rubric-guided aggregation into interpretable multi-dimensional scores. We instantiate FLY-EVAL++ for Flight Trajectory and Attitude Prediction (FTAP) by extending the PilotBench setting with history-conditioned and multi-step prediction tasks. Across 66 LLMs, safety compliance is the most discriminative dimension of model behavior: models with comparable predictive performance differ by more than 28 points in safety score, and we observe recurrent failures including safety violations under physically plausible predictions and instability in multi-step rollouts. These results show that evaluation in safety-critical domains should measure constraint satisfaction and structured validity explicitly rather than rely on accuracy-centric reporting alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FLY-EVAL++：一种基于证据的安全约束飞行预测评估协议
在安全关键、物理约束的环境中评估大型语言模型（LLMs）需要的不仅仅是基于准确性的指标，因为数值上接近真实值的预测仍可能违反操作约束、以物理不一致的方式组合领域，或未能产生可用的结构化输出。现有的评估协议无法可靠地测量这些失败模式。我们提出了FLY-EVAL++，一种基于证据的评估协议，结合了对协议合规性、物理可行性和安全约束的确定性验证，以及通过固定评分标准引导的聚合，形成可解释的多维评分。我们通过扩展PilotBench设置，结合历史条件和多步预测任务，为飞行轨迹和姿态预测（FTAP）实例化FLY-EVAL++。在66个LLM中，安全合规性是模型行为中最具区分性的维度：具有可比预测性能的模型在安全评分上相差超过28分，我们观察到反复出现的失败，包括在物理合理预测下的安全违规和多步展开中的不稳定性。这些结果表明，在安全关键领域的评估应明确测量约束满足和结构有效性，而不是仅依赖于以准确性为中心的报告。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the need for effective evaluation protocols for large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical environments, where traditional accuracy-based metrics are insufficient due to the potential for operational violations and inconsistencies in predictions. Previous methods have failed to reliably assess these failure modes, prompting the development of FLY-EVAL++, an evidence-driven evaluation protocol that integrates deterministic verification of compliance with safety constraints and physical feasibility, along with structured scoring. The contribution of this paper lies in its novel approach to evaluating Flight Trajectory and Attitude Prediction (FTAP) tasks, demonstrating that safety compliance is a critical dimension of model performance, with significant discrepancies observed among models despite similar predictive accuracy. The methodology involves applying FLY-EVAL++ across 66 LLMs, revealing recurrent failures and emphasizing the necessity of measuring constraint satisfaction and structured validity in safety-critical evaluations, thus supporting the goal of enhancing model reliability in such domains.
本文探讨了在安全关键环境中有效评估大型语言模型（LLMs）的必要性，传统的基于准确性的指标不足以应对潜在的操作违规和物理不一致的输出。以往的方法未能可靠地评估这些失败模式，因此提出了FLY-EVAL++，一种证据驱动的评估协议，结合了对安全约束和物理可行性的合规性进行确定性验证，以及结构化评分系统。该工作的贡献在于能够提供对LLMs更细致的评估，特别是在飞行轨迹和姿态预测（FTAP）的背景下，揭示了具有相似预测性能的模型在安全合规性方面存在显著差异。该方法论通过扩展PilotBench设置，纳入历史条件和多步预测任务，表明安全合规性是模型行为的关键维度，研究结果表明评估应优先考虑约束满足和结构有效性，而不仅仅依赖于准确性。
TACS: Trajectory-Aware Candidate Selection for LLM Jailbreak Suffix Optimization
Authors: Shiliang Xiao
First: 2026-08-30T05:18:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-01T03:40:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gradient-based jailbreak suffix optimization methods typically update the suffix by retaining the candidate with the lowest current loss. We show that this seemingly natural design is fundamentally myopic: candidates that look better under the current-step proxy often fail to produce better jailbreak outcomes later in the search, revealing a form of selection-stage reward hacking. This suggests that candidate selection, rather than candidate generation alone, is a hidden bottleneck in suffix optimization. To address this issue, we propose TACS, a trajectory-aware candidate selection framework for jailbreak suffix optimization. Instead of selecting candidates solely by their immediate loss, TACS augments per-step evaluation with a trajectory-aware proxy and stabilizes selection with reference-policy regularization and a discriminator-estimated chi-squared correction, encouraging choices that remain effective beyond the current step. Experiments on HarmBench show that TACS consistently outperforms strong baselines under the same search budget, substantially improving attack success rates while exhibiting more stable optimization behavior throughout the search. Our findings highlight that mitigating selection-stage reward hacking caused by myopic candidate selection is critical for improving jailbreak suffix optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TACS：用于LLM越狱后缀优化的轨迹感知候选选择
基于梯度的越狱后缀优化方法通常通过保留当前损失最低的候选者来更新后缀。我们表明，这种看似自然的设计在根本上是短视的：在当前步骤代理下看起来更好的候选者往往在搜索的后期未能产生更好的越狱结果，揭示了一种选择阶段奖励黑客行为。这表明，候选选择而非仅仅候选生成是后缀优化中的一个隐藏瓶颈。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了TACS，一个用于越狱后缀优化的轨迹感知候选选择框架。TACS不仅仅通过候选者的即时损失来选择候选者，而是通过轨迹感知代理增强每一步的评估，并通过参考策略正则化和判别器估计的卡方修正来稳定选择，鼓励在当前步骤之外仍然有效的选择。在HarmBench上的实验表明，TACS在相同搜索预算下始终优于强基线，显著提高了攻击成功率，同时在整个搜索过程中表现出更稳定的优化行为。我们的研究结果强调，减轻由短视候选选择引起的选择阶段奖励黑客行为对于改善越狱后缀优化至关重要。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of existing gradient-based jailbreak suffix optimization methods, which typically select candidates based solely on the lowest current loss, leading to suboptimal long-term outcomes due to a phenomenon known as selection-stage reward hacking. The proposed approach, TACS, introduces a trajectory-aware candidate selection framework that enhances the evaluation process by incorporating a trajectory-aware proxy and stabilizing selection through reference-policy regularization and a discriminator-estimated chi-squared correction. This method effectively mitigates the issues associated with myopic candidate selection, resulting in improved attack success rates and more stable optimization behavior. Experiments conducted on HarmBench demonstrate that TACS consistently outperforms strong baseline methods within the same search budget, confirming its efficacy in enhancing jailbreak suffix optimization.
本研究解决了现有基于梯度的越狱后缀优化方法的局限性，这些方法通常仅依赖于根据即时损失选择候选者，导致由于选择阶段奖励黑客行为而产生的次优长期结果。所提出的方法TACS引入了一种基于轨迹的候选选择框架，通过结合轨迹感知代理和通过参考策略正则化及鉴别器估计的卡方校正来增强评估过程，从而稳定选择。该方法有效缓解了与短视候选选择相关的问题，导致性能提升。在HarmBench上进行的实验表明，TACS在相同搜索预算内显著优于既定基线，取得了更高的攻击成功率，并在整个搜索过程中表现出更稳定的优化行为，从而支持了增强越狱后缀优化的目标。
Validity-Aware Jailbreak Evaluation for Large Language Models
Authors: Qilong Wu, Sahil Wadhwa, Pranab Mohanty, Giri Iyengar, Varun Chandrasekaran
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-08-31T23:57:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-31T23:57:53+00:00
Comments: To appear on EMNLP 2026 main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Jailbreak robustness has become central to large language model (LLM) safety evaluation, yet prevailing methodologies rely primarily on refusal behavior, semantic resemblance, and intent-matching heuristics that emphasize linguistic plausibility rather than correctness. We identify a key limitation in existing evaluations: many jailbreak intents depend on instructional validity rather than epistemic factuality, allowing realistic-looking responses to be labeled successful despite being factually or procedurally incorrect. To address this gap, we propose Sequential Epistemic and Action-Level Validation (SEAV), a verification-centric jailbreak evaluation framework that decomposes responses into ordered steps and evaluates both validity and correctness. SEAV combines LLM-as-a-judge mechanisms for semantic interpretation with retrieval-grounded verification using external knowledge sources, assessing whether generated content is factually correct, structurally consistent, and operationally capable of advancing harmful objectives. Empirically, SEAV cuts the false-positive rate on SD-A (a curated strategic-dishonesty diagnostic) by 14.9\,pp vs. the strongest baseline, and reclassifies 22.1\%--51.0\% of sampled prior-labeled successes as invalid across three of four public benchmarks. Together, these results show that enforcing correctness substantially reshapes measured robustness: many previously labeled jailbreak successes are reclassified as invalid, and results are stable across the tested search backends and evaluator models. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Ardor-Wu/SEAV.

标题：针对大型语言模型的有效性感知越狱评估
越狱的鲁棒性已成为大型语言模型（LLM）安全评估的核心，但现有方法主要依赖于拒绝行为、语义相似性和意图匹配启发式，这些方法强调语言的合理性而非正确性。我们识别出现有评估的一个关键限制：许多越狱意图依赖于指令有效性而非认知事实性，允许看似真实的响应被标记为成功，尽管在事实或程序上是错误的。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了顺序认知和行动级验证（SEAV），这是一个以验证为中心的越狱评估框架，它将响应分解为有序步骤，并评估有效性和正确性。SEAV结合了LLM作为评判机制的语义解释与使用外部知识源的检索基础验证，评估生成内容是否在事实上正确、结构上连贯，并在操作上能够推进有害目标。实证结果表明，SEAV将SD-A（一个策划的战略不诚实诊断）的假阳性率降低了14.9个百分点，相较于最强基线，并在四个公共基准中的三个重新将22.1%至51.0%的先前标记为成功的样本重新分类为无效。这些结果表明，强制执行正确性显著重塑了测量的鲁棒性：许多先前标记为越狱成功的案例被重新分类为无效，且结果在测试的搜索后端和评估模型中保持稳定。代码和数据可在https://github.com/Ardor-Wu/SEAV获取。

Risk-Adjusted Harm Scoring for Automated Red Teaming for LLMs in Financial Services
Authors: Fabrizio Dimino, Bhaskarjit Sarmah, Stefano Pasquali
First: 2026-03-11T14:14:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-31T14:24:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing LLM safety evaluations rely on binary attack-success rates and domain-agnostic taxonomies, leaving regulated Banking, Financial Services, and Insurance (BFSI) deployments exposed to failures elicited through legally or professionally plausible framing. We introduce RAHS (Risk-Adjusted Harm Score), a risk-sensitive metric jointly capturing disclosure severity, disclaimer mitigation, and inter-judge agreement, and FinRedTeamBench, a 989-prompt benchmark spanning seven BFSI risk areas and 34 sub-categories mapped to regulatory frameworks. Evaluation uses an ensemble of three heterogeneous LLM judges, validated against human experts, and an adaptive multi-turn red-teaming pipeline. On nine open-weight models, RAHS preserves separation under near-ceiling ASR, ranking is stable under hyperparameter sweeps, and multi-turn pressure drives not only more jailbreaks but more operationally severe disclosures, exposing failure modes that single-turn, domain-agnostic evaluations cannot reveal.

Do We Still Need Humans in the Loop? Human vs. LLM Annotation in Active Learning for TikTok Hate Speech Detection
Authors: Ahmad Dawar Hakimi, Lea Hirlimann, Isabelle Augenstein, Hinrich Schütze
First: 2026-04-15T14:10:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-31T11:56:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Annotating data remains a costly bottleneck for supervised NLP. Active learning (AL) reduces the number of human labels needed by selecting only the most informative instances, while instruction-tuned LLMs attack the same bottleneck from the other side, making labels cheap enough to annotate entire corpora. This raises two questions: can LLM labels replace human labels within the AL loop, and does AL remain necessary when entire corpora can be cheaply labeled? We investigate both by training supervised hate speech classifiers on a new dataset of 278K German political TikTok comments, comparing human and LLM annotation under matched conditions. LLM annotation at scale outperforms human-supervised classifiers at roughly one-tenth the cost, for both a closed-source (GPT-5.2) and an open-weight (Qwen3.5-122B-A10B) LLM, and the advantage is robust under soft-label evaluation. It hinges on the annotation interface: only a two-question decomposition mirroring the human annotation task unlocks it. AL provides no reliable advantage over random sampling in our prefiltered pool. Error structure depends on the LLM: only GPT-5.2 matches the human FP/FN balance, while other variants over-flag border-control and economic-competition discourse. Humans remain essential as evaluators; for training labels, the question shifts to which LLM, which interface, and what shape of pool.

From Detection to Refusal: Safer LLMs via Circuit-Guided Weight Scaling
Authors: Kuan-Lin Chu, Chung-En Sun, Tsui-Wei Weng
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-08-30T08:01:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-30T08:01:42+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Findings of the 2026 Conference on Empirical Methods in Natural Language Processing (EMNLP 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite extensive alignment efforts, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to generating unsafe content under adversarial prompting, yet the internal mechanisms by which safety behaviors are implemented remain poorly understood. We study LLM safety from a mechanistic interpretability perspective and characterize a multi-stage *safety circuit* that organizes refusal behavior, consisting of (i) $\textbf{Harmful Detection Heads}$ that respond to harmful inputs, (ii) $\textbf{Safety Neurons}$ that mediate and stabilize safety signals in the residual stream, and (iii) $\textbf{Refusal Heads}$ that translate these signals into safe response generation. Using targeted attention-head and neuron-level interventions, we provide causal evidence consistent with this circuit organization, showing that suppressing upstream Harmful Detection Heads disrupts downstream refusal behavior and that safety neurons mediate this interaction. We validate that this decomposition recurs across multiple LLM architectures and adversarial attack settings, and use simple, architecture-preserving weight scaling as a mechanistic probe to test its functional relevance. Across six LLMs, circuit-guided scaling improves safety rates under attacks by 26.5%, while incurring only a 1.7% accuracy drop across four standard benchmarks. Overall, our results support a circuit-level interpretation of LLM safety and suggest that mechanistic abstractions can reveal stable and transferable patterns underlying aligned behavior.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从检测到拒绝：通过电路引导的权重缩放实现更安全的LLM
尽管进行了广泛的对齐努力，大型语言模型（LLM）在对抗性提示下仍然容易生成不安全内容，但实现安全行为的内部机制仍然不够清楚。我们从机制可解释性的角度研究LLM安全性，并描述一个多阶段的*安全电路*，该电路组织拒绝行为，包括（i）$\textbf{有害检测头}$，响应有害输入，（ii）$\textbf{安全神经元}$，在残差流中介导和稳定安全信号，以及（iii）$\textbf{拒绝头}$，将这些信号转化为安全响应生成。通过针对性注意力头和神经元级干预，我们提供了与该电路组织一致的因果证据，表明抑制上游有害检测头会干扰下游拒绝行为，而安全神经元介导了这种互动。我们验证了这种分解在多个LLM架构和对抗攻击设置中反复出现，并使用简单的、保持架构的权重缩放作为机制探针来测试其功能相关性。在六个LLM中，电路引导的缩放在攻击下提高了26.5%的安全率，同时在四个标准基准上仅造成1.7%的准确率下降。总体而言，我们的结果支持LLM安全性的电路级解释，并表明机制抽象可以揭示对齐行为背后的稳定和可转移模式。

The proposed approach introduces a multi-stage safety circuit that includes Harmful Detection Heads, Safety Neurons, and Refusal Heads, which collectively enhance the model's ability to refuse unsafe prompts. The research methodology involves targeted interventions at the attention-head and neuron levels, demonstrating causal relationships within the safety circuit. The findings show that circuit-guided weight scaling improves safety rates by 26.5% under adversarial attacks while maintaining a minimal accuracy drop of 1.7% across standard benchmarks, thus supporting the goal of enhancing LLM safety effectively.
